A Letter For You 💌

Dear Riya,

I've been trying to find the right way to say this, and honestly? I'm just gonna go for it.

From when we first met, I knew there was something special about you. The way you laugh, the way you see things — everything about you just gets to me.

You're the kind of person who makes me want to be better. And I really, really love you.

— Gohith 💕
